Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending `.md` to the page URL.<br>## List models<br>**get** `/models`<br>Lists the currently available models, and provides basic information about each one such as the owner and availability.<br>### Returns<br>- `data: array of Model`<br>- `id: string`<br>The model identifier, which can be referenced in the API endpoints.<br>- `created: integer`<br>The Unix timestamp (in seconds) when the model was created.<br>`format: unixtime`<br>- `object: "model"`<br>The object type, which is always "model".<br>- `"model"`<br>- `owned_by: string`<br>The organization that owns the model.<br>- `shutdown_date: optional string or null`<br>The date when the model will shut down, or null if not announced.<br>`format: date`<br>- `object: "list"`<br>- `"list"`<br>### Example<br>```http<br>curl https://api.openai.com/v1/models \<br>-H "Authorization: Bearer $OPENAI_API_KEY"<br>```<br>#### Response example<br>```json<br>{<br>"object": "list",<br>"data": [<br>{<br>"id": "model-id-0",<br>"object": "model",<br>"created": 1686935002,<br>"owned_by": "organization-owner",<br>"shutdown_date": null<br>},<br>{<br>"id": "model-id-1",<br>"object": "model",<br>"created": 1686935002,<br>"owned_by": "organization-owner",<br>"shutdown_date": null<br>},<br>{<br>"id": "model-id-2",<br>"object": "model",<br>"created": 1686935002,<br>"owned_by": "openai",<br>"shutdown_date": "2026-10-23"<br>}<br>]<br>}<br>``` |

## openai 专属证据与核对说明

### OpenAI 官方通用资料采集候选

- 来源快照：11 份
- 文档观察：11 份；模型 ID 提及：291 条
- 已生成 Offering：107 个

#### 适配器核对边界

- 页面线索留在文档观察区；目录提及不等于当前供应，价格线索不等于已解析的计费规则。
- 每个来源的覆盖状态为「尚未解析」，需由 Provider 专属解析逻辑形成字段事实后才能进入通用投影。
- 不调用需 API Key、Cookie 或登录会话的接口，不执行推理请求，也不采集单模型专属页面。

#### 已知边界

- GET /v1/models 返回认证账号可见范围，不调用需 Key 的真实接口。
- 公开模型文档混有 API、ChatGPT、预览/弃用与推荐模型范围；候选只记录文档 ID 提及，不能用它充当可调用全集。
- 模型目录和价格按精确 Provider 模型 ID 记录；规范模型规格由维护者手工维护。
